# Agents · S · part 21

885 entries, seq-logging-specialist to session-scribe. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **seq-logging-specialist**: Seq и structured logging - log analysis, correlation, error tracking, alerting. Handoff - принимает задачу/симптом + опц. `mode` и `deploy` (санкция на state-changing операции), отдаёт диагностику + результат либо подготовленную операцию. Триггеры - seq logs, find errors, log analysis, correlation id, structured logging, serilog, log level, error tracking, seq query, логи, ошибки в логах, корреляция ([dex-it/claude-code-marketplace](https://github.com/dex-it/claude-code-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/specialists/infrastructure/dex-logging-seq/agents/seq-logging-specialist.md) · ★2)
- **seqeron-cartographer**: Survey the Seqeron repository and propose a shortlist of specific, evidence-grounded skill candidates that fill real gaps in skill coverage. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent at Phase 0. Reads copilot-instructions.md and the real skill surface (existing skills, tool docs, algorithm docs, STRATEGY.md), then emits .skill-dev/skill-candidates.json with 5–12 candidates, each carrying a senior-engineer-legible problem_statement, its target_files, and Impact/Frequency/Convention-density/Authoring-cost signals for the orchestrator's triage. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-cartographer.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-coherence-auditor**: Audit the FULL installed Seqeron skill set for coherence — colliding/overlapping triggers, capability gaps versus the 11 MCP servers/domains, convention drift (dual-mode, provenance, link-depth, delegate-to-bio-rigor), clean domain boundaries, and byte-identical parity between the .claude/skills and .github/skills trees. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent at Phase 4, after all candidates have shipped. Produces .skill-dev/coherence-report.md with a paste-ready top-level summary and severity-tagged findings carrying concrete human follow-ups. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-coherence-auditor.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-domain-researcher**: Produces the grounded research pack for ONE Seqeron skill candidate. Invoked as a subagent by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator (Step 2.1). Reads the candidate from .skill-dev/skill-candidates.json and emits a research-pack/ (conventions.md + citations.md + supporting notes) built ONLY from verified repo docs — every tool name and Method ID cited to a doc path, none invented. Emits escalation.md to halt a candidate whose scope cannot be grounded. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-domain-researcher.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-dual-target-verifier**: Verify a draft Seqeron skill for Claude-Code + GitHub-Copilot/VS-Code dual-runtime compatibility. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent (Step 2.4). Checks frontmatter, dual-mode content, progressive disclosure, relative-link depth, and link resolution against .github/copilot-instructions.md, then produces compat-report.md with explicit PASS/FAIL checks the orchestrator loops on. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-dual-target-verifier.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-eval-architect**: Design the eval set for one candidate Seqeron skill. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent (Step 2.6). Reads the draft SKILL.md frontmatter + trigger-attacks.json and writes .skill-dev/&lt;id&gt;/evals/evals.json — a balanced set of positive/negative/ambiguous trigger evals with strict, checkable quality assertions the eval runner can grade. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-eval-architect.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-eval-runner**: Execute the eval set for one Seqeron skill end-to-end inside VS Code. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent. For each eval prompt, simulates a fresh session, determines whether the skill would trigger, and grades the assertions against a generated response. Produces results.json with verdicts. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-eval-runner.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-skill-author**: Authors and revises a single Seqeron SKILL.md draft (plus draft/references/) from a candidate's research pack. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent in three modes — initial authoring (Step 2.2), targeted compat-fixes (Step 2.5), and a description-only revision pass (Step 2.7). Writes ONLY under .skill-dev/&lt;candidate_id&gt;/draft/; never installs into .claude/skills/ or .github/skills/. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-skill-author.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-skill-pipeline**: End-to-end orchestrator for the Seqeron skill development pipeline. This is the ONLY agent the human invokes directly. It runs all phases — discovery, triage, per-candidate authoring, evaluation, coherence audit — by delegating to specialist subagents via runSubagent. Use this agent whenever the user asks "develop the missing skills", "run the skill pipeline", or anything similar. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-skill-pipeline.agent.md) · ★5)
- **seqeron-trigger-adversary**: Adversarially attacks a Seqeron skill's trigger surface — its frontmatter description — to surface false positives (realistic prompts a loose description would wrongly catch) and false negatives (realistic prompts a too-narrow description would miss). Reads ONLY the YAML frontmatter, never the body. Invoked by the seqeron-skill-pipeline orchestrator as a subagent; produces trigger-attacks.json for the description-revision pass and the eval architect. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/seqeron-trigger-adversary.agent.md) · ★5)

- **sequential-pipeline-predictability-and-parallel-execution-efficiency-are-in-direct-tension-for-sdlc-build-loops**: Sequential processing is predictable and debuggable but serializes independent tasks that could run concurrently; parallel execution reduces wall-clock time but requires dependency graph analysis to be safe — this tension cannot be dissolved in v1, where sequentialism is the deliberate trade-off accepting higher wall-clock time in exchange for lower coordination risk. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/sequential-pipeline-predictability-and-parallel-execution-efficiency-are-in-direct-tension-for-sdlc-build-loops.md))
- **sequential-pipeline-with-backward-iteration-loops-is-the-lower-risk-v1-architecture-for-multi-agent-build-loops**: A sequential pipeline that processes SDLC phases in fixed order, combined with explicit backward iteration loops within each phase, is the lower-risk first architecture for multi-agent build loops — it delivers predictability and debuggability before parallel execution or hierarchical delegation are introduced. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/sequential-pipeline-with-backward-iteration-loops-is-the-lower-risk-v1-architecture-for-multi-agent-build-loops.md))

- **session-auditor**: Session-history analyst — indexes session logs and extracts turns, tool-call patterns, recurring work, and lessons; use for workflow profiling and post-incident audits ([nurmdrafi/pi-native](https://github.com/nurmdrafi/pi-native/blob/HEAD/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★4)
- **session-auditor**: USE WHEN: 用户调 `/session-review` 想看当前 session 工作流执行情况(flow 推进 / agent 边界 / blocker 处理)。OUTPUT: 审查报告(只读) 或 flow 配置修复(`--fix`)。DO NOT USE: 修业务代码(只改 flow 配置) / 跨 session 趋势审查(走 workflow-reviewer) / 单纯查 task 状态(直接读 task.json)。 ([dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow](https://github.com/dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **session-auditor**: Read-only Pi session audit specialist for recent-session reviews, workflow friction analysis, tool-error metrics, cost and token analysis, and evidence-backed extension, skill, agent, or instruction improvements ([adaminglehart/pi-config](https://github.com/adaminglehart/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Orchestrates one Claude Code session's audit end to end -- runs the cost and timeline extractors, fans out to 5 general-purpose shards (4 parallel, 1 sequential), merges their digests, and renders the self-contained audit_session-&lt;sid&gt;.html. ([brunogsa/unix-utils](https://github.com/brunogsa/unix-utils/blob/HEAD/configs/ai-docs/claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Reviews what changed in a session and checks against CLAUDE.md rules ([Khaledaun/Yalla-london](https://github.com/Khaledaun/Yalla-london/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Session audit critic; dispatched with ROLE=bug_hunt or ROLE=claim_bust. Read-only adversarial lens on invoking-thread deliverables. Returns structured findings only — no writes, no fixes. ([mrkinglollipop/manna-links](https://github.com/mrkinglollipop/manna-links/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Audits a Claude Code session transcript for token spend and harness-usage quality — wasted calls, missed delegation, cache misses — and writes a graded retrospective. Use after a session ends. ([prototypdigital/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/prototypdigital/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/backend/agents/session-auditor.md) · 10 copies)
- **session-auditor**: Audit Pi SDK session storage for unbounded growth, missing TTLs, and cross-tenant data leaks. ([srbryers/Yumi](https://github.com/srbryers/Yumi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))

- **session-boundary-agent**: 判断是否到达 session 边界（该 checkpoint/compact/clear/branch/换 reviewer）并维护 session-tree 时使用。 ([kanydao/ml-template](https://github.com/kanydao/ml-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-boundary-agent.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **session-bridge**: Cross-session context persistence — auto-saves decisions, conventions, progress, instincts to .rune/ files. Loads + integrity-checks at session start. Auto-triggered before compaction. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge.md) · ★93)
- **session-bridge-agent**: Use when a subagent should save or load cross-session state in .topia/ (decisions, conventions, progress), for example before compaction or at session start. Runs the session-bridge skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge-agent.md) · ★1)
- **session-brief**: What every agent session on this project needs to know. A session prompt names only the issue and what is specific to it; the rest is here. Shared rules live in the workflow kit and are linked, not copied. ([DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector](https://github.com/DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/session-brief.md))
- **session-brief**: Reads process docs, architecture docs, active plans, and the last commit to produce a concise session brief. Run at the start of any session to get up to speed without re-exploring the codebase. ([SoapyD/3D_Map_Generator](https://github.com/SoapyD/3D_Map_Generator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_session-brief.md))
- **session-brief-agent**: Haiku-powered context cache producing a 24h-cached per-account session brief. Consumed by orchestrator and workflow skills' Phase 0 context loading to reduce redundant per-day reads. Plugin-scoped, never invoked directly by user. ([migos1990/presales-harness-starter](https://github.com/migos1990/presales-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/session-brief-agent.md) · ★2)
- **session-briefer**: Comprehensive session briefings for instant context understanding. Triggers on "get up to speed with @sessions/[SESSION_NAME]" or "brief me on @sessions/[SESSION_NAME]". ([elevanaltd/eav-cep-assist](https://github.com/elevanaltd/eav-cep-assist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-briefer.oct.md) · 2 copies)
- **session-briefer**: Briefeur de session CYNA — lit le graphe mémoire au démarrage et produit un briefing complet : état actuel, tâches en attente, risques actifs, patterns à surveiller. Invoquer en PREMIER à chaque nouvelle session de travail. ([Valdrin72/gestion-chantier](https://github.com/Valdrin72/gestion-chantier/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-briefer.md))
- **session-buddy-specialist**: Use proactively for anything touching the Session-Buddy MCP server — its 49 tools, the Phase 1/1.5 signer feed, the Phase 3 agent catalog, the reflection database, and the lifespan-owned ``SkillsSigner``. Routes through ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_list_skills`` and ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_get_skill`` for skill discovery and through ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_list_agents`` plus ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_get_agent`` for agent installs. Self-published from the session-buddy server (genuinely new — no adjacent specialist in the ecosystem; see Scope). ([lesleslie/session-buddy](https://github.com/lesleslie/session-buddy/blob/HEAD/session_buddy/mcp/agents/session-buddy-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **session-budget**: Session length is governed by CONTEXT SIZE, never by a task count. Re-reading a long context is the orchestrator's largest cost, and a session that fills mid-task is the hardest one to stop. So plan the work so that you are always one **clean logical break** away from stopping: all work committed, the tracker current, no sub-agent running. ([Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit](https://github.com/Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit/blob/HEAD/templates/marvin/agents/session-budget.md))
- **session-checkpoint**: Use this agent when context usage exceeds approximately 50% capacity and you need to prepare for a `/clear` to continue work in a fresh session. Also use this agent when a significant milestone is reached mid-session and you want to capture progress before moving on to the next phase. This agent reads the current plan, completed work, and remaining tasks to produce a structured checkpoint file — it never modifies source code.\n\nExamples:\n\n- Example 1:\n Context: The user has been working on implementing F5 shortlist feature and context is getting long.\n user: "Let's continue implementing… ([milos-agathon/buurt-check](https://github.com/milos-agathon/buurt-check/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-checkpoint.md) · ★4)
- **session-classifier**: Classifies Claude Code session digests into fixed facets for the sessions-to-fluency skill. Use only when that skill hands over a batch of digests. ([Mesh-AI/agentic-engineering-fluency-plugin](https://github.com/Mesh-AI/agentic-engineering-fluency-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-engineering-fluency/agents/session-classifier.md))
- **session-close**: Run when the user says "wrap up". Writes an achievement tracking entry to the project's Obsidian vault. Encodes corrections as CLAUDE.md rules and memory entries. Creates any new agents or skills identified during the session and pushes them to the scaffold repo. Takes a structured brief from the invoking Claude instance. Does not write session summaries or update workstream notes - those are maintained during the session. ([cjeggo/claude-code](https://github.com/cjeggo/claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/session-close.md))
- **session-close-background-work-handover-2026-10-03**: Research synthesis, 2026-10-03. Question: how should a multi-session Claude Code setup (a coordinator, a watcher or a fan-out lane) close a session without losing its background work, and can a successor session take that work over? - **session-ids**: By the end of this guide, every LLM request from your agent carries a session ID, and subagent requests point to their parent.
